<!-- example-1: body_md -->
{ex.setup.cv}
\,

Let $W = (Y,X)$, with outcome $Y$ and covariates $X$, and let $\eta = (\mu, \nu)$ be a pair of predictors of $Y$ from $X$. 
The fitted model ${\hat{\eta}}_k = ({\hat{\mu}}_k, {\hat{\nu}}_k)$ then consists of two predictors trained on fold $\mathsf{s}_k$ by different learners, for example a random forest and a neural network. 
The score is the loss difference 
$$f_{\eta}(y,x) = L(y, \mu(x)) - L(y, \nu(x)),$$
where $L$ is a loss function, such as the squared error $L(y,\hat{y}) = (y - \hat{y})^2$ or the classification error $L(y,\hat{y}) =  
\IfNoValueTF{#1}{\operatorname{\mathds{1}}} 
{\mathds{1}\mathopen{}{\left( #1 \right)}}{y \neq \hat{y}}$ for discrete outcomes. 

Each 
$$\theta_{{\hat{\eta}}_k} = \int \left[ L(y, {\hat{\mu}}_k(x)) - L(y, {\hat{\nu}}_k(x)) \right] \mathrm{d} P(y,x)$$
is the difference in out-of-sample loss between the two fitted models ${\hat{\mu}}_k$ and ${\hat{\nu}}_k$, and the estimand 
$$\theta_{\hat{\eta}} = \frac{1}{2} \left( \theta_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} + \theta_{{\hat{\eta}}_2} \right)$$
is its average over the two folds, or, equivalently, the out-of-sample loss difference when $({\hat{\mu}}_k, {\hat{\nu}}_k)$ is drawn uniformly at random from the two fitted pairs.
<!-- end example-1 -->
<!-- example-2: body_md -->
{ex.setup.otr}
\,

Let $W = (Y, A, X)$, with outcome $Y$, binary treatment assignment $A$, and covariates $X$, in a randomized experiment with known treatment probability $ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{P}}\IfValueT{A = 1 \mid X}{_{A = 1 \mid X}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{P}}\IfValueT{A = 1 \mid X}{_{A = 1 \mid X}}\mathopen{}{\left( #2 \right)}} = \pi \in (0,1)$.
Moreover, let $Y(0), Y(1)$ denote potential outcomes with $Y = Y(A)$, and let $\eta : \mathcal{X} \to \left\lbrace 0,1 \right\rbrace$ be a treatment rule, so that ${\hat{\eta}}_k$ is a rule learned on fold $\mathsf{s}_k$, for example by empirical welfare maximization \citep{kitagawaWhoShouldBe2018} or by thresholding an estimate of the conditional average treatment effect $\tau(x) =  
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{Y(1) - Y(0) \mid X = x}{_{Y(1) - Y(0) \mid X = x}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{Y(1) - Y(0) \mid X = x}{_{Y(1) - Y(0) \mid X = x}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}$.

The value of a rule is $V(\eta) =  
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{Y(\eta(X))}{_{Y(\eta(X))}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{Y(\eta(X))}{_{Y(\eta(X))}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}$, and $V(\eta) = P f_\eta$ for
$$f_\eta(y, a, x) = \frac{a y}{\pi} \eta(x) + \frac{(1-a) y}{1 - \pi} \left\lbrace 1 - \eta(x) \right\rbrace.$$
Each $\theta_{{\hat{\eta}}_k} = V({\hat{\eta}}_k)$ is the value of the rule learned on fold $\mathsf{s}_k$, and the estimand $\theta_{\hat{\eta}}$ is the average value of the two learned rules.

The value of the learned treatment regimes is also informative for the value of the optimal treatment regime. 
The learned rules are dominated by the optimal rule $\eta^*(x) =  
\IfNoValueTF{#1}{\operatorname{\mathds{1}}} 
{\mathds{1}\mathopen{}{\left( #1 \right)}}{\tau(x) > 0}$, so $\theta_{\hat{\eta}} \le V(\eta^*)$, and a lower confidence bound for $\theta_{\hat{\eta}}$ is also a lower confidence bound for the optimal value.
Moreover, inference on $\theta_{\hat{\eta}}$ is asymptotically equivalent to inference on $V(\eta^*)$ whenever the regret $V(\eta^*) - \theta_{\hat{\eta}} = o_p(n^{-1/2})$, which \citet{luedtkeStatisticalInferenceMean2016} establish under margin and rate conditions, without requiring ${\hat{\eta}}_k$ to converge.
<!-- end example-2 -->
<!-- example-3: body_md -->
{ex.setup.blp}
\,

Let $W = (Y, X)$, and let $\eta : \mathcal{X} \to \mathbb{R}$ be a predictor of $Y$ from $X$.
A natural way to assess the predictive power and calibration of ${\hat{\eta}}_k$ is to regress $Y$ on ${\hat{\eta}}_k(X)$ and a constant in the test fold ${\tilde{\mathsf{s}}}_k$. 
A slope of zero means no predictive power, and a slope of one means good calibration. 

Holding ${\hat{\eta}}_k$ fixed, the population slope is 
$$\theta_{{\hat{\eta}}_k} = Pf_{{\hat{\eta}}_k}, \qquad f_\eta(y, x) = \frac{(y -  
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{Y}{_{Y}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{Y}{_{Y}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]})\left\lbrace \eta(x) - P\eta \right\rbrace}{P(\eta - P\eta)^2}.$$ 
The ordinary least squares estimator differs from $\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_k}$ due to the oracle scale $P({\hat{\eta}}_k - P{\hat{\eta}}_k)^2$ and the oracle centerings $ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{Y}{_{Y}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{Y}{_{Y}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}$ and $P\eta$, but this distinction is typically first-order negligible since the sample variance of ${\hat{\eta}}_k(X)$ is ratio-consistent for $P({\hat{\eta}}_k - P{\hat{\eta}}_k)^2$ under mild moment conditions. 
Under the null that $ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{Y \mid X}{_{Y \mid X}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{Y \mid X}{_{Y \mid X}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}$ is constant, if ${\hat{\eta}}_k$ is consistent for this constant, $P({\hat{\eta}}_k - P{\hat{\eta}}_k)^2 \overset{p}{\to} 0$, and the score diverges. 
The standardized score $(f_{{\hat{\eta}}_k} - P f_{{\hat{\eta}}_k}) / \operatorname{Var}[{f_{{\hat{\eta}}_k}(W)}]^{1/2}$ may then have no deterministic limit, which is the nonregularity my CLT addresses.

The same construction tests for treatment effect heterogeneity in a randomized controlled trial by replacing $Y$ with its Horvitz--Thompson transform, which yields the best linear predictor of \citet[Theorem~3.2]{chernozhukovFisherSchultzLecture2025a}.
<!-- end example-3 -->
<!-- assumption-1: body_md -->
{ass.clt_2cf}
\,
For some $\bar{c} < \infty$, $\underaccent{\bar}{c} > 0$, $p \in (2, \infty]$, with the convention $1/p = 0$ when $p = \infty$, and sufficiently large $n$, the following conditions hold:
\begin{assumptionenum}
    \item \label{ass.clt_2cf.moments} (Moments). 
    $$\left\lVert f_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}(W) \right\rVert_p \le \bar{c};$$
    \item \label{ass.clt_2cf.degeneracy} (Non-degeneracy). 
    $$\rho_n \ge -1 + \underaccent{\bar}{c};$$
    \item \label{ass.clt_2cf.stability} (Mean-square stability). 
    $$n^{\frac{1}{2(1-1/p)}} \gamma_n \to 0;$$
    \item \label{ass.clt_2cf.locality} (Locality).
    $$ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Cov}\IfValueT{r_1, r_2}{_{r_1, r_2}}} 
{\operatorname{Cov}\IfValueT{r_1, r_2}{_{r_1, r_2}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]} \to 0.$$
\end{assumptionenum}
<!-- end assumption-1 -->
<!-- lemma-1: body_md -->
\label{lem.clt_2cf.sufficient}
    When $ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{r_1}{_{r_1}}} 
{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{r_1}{_{r_1}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]} > 0$, 
    $$\left\lvert  
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Cov}\IfValueT{r_1, r_2}{_{r_1, r_2}}} 
{\operatorname{Cov}\IfValueT{r_1, r_2}{_{r_1, r_2}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]} \right\rvert \le n \gamma_n \left\lvert  
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Corr}\IfValueT{r_1, r_2}{_{r_1, r_2}}} 
{\operatorname{Corr}\IfValueT{r_1, r_2}{_{r_1, r_2}}\mathopen{}{\left( #2 \right)}} \right\rvert.$$
    In particular, \Cref{ass.clt_2cf.stability,ass.clt_2cf.locality} hold under any of the following:
    \begin{enumerate}[label=\upshape(\alph*)]
        \item  
        $n \gamma_n = O(1),  
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Corr}\IfValueT{r_1, r_2}{_{r_1, r_2}}} 
{\operatorname{Corr}\IfValueT{r_1, r_2}{_{r_1, r_2}}\mathopen{}{\left( #2 \right)}} = o(1)$;
        \item  
        $p = \infty, \sqrt{n} \, \gamma_n = o(1), \sqrt{n} \,  
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Corr}\IfValueT{r_1, r_2}{_{r_1, r_2}}} 
{\operatorname{Corr}\IfValueT{r_1, r_2}{_{r_1, r_2}}\mathopen{}{\left( #2 \right)}} = O(1)$;
        \item  
        $n \gamma_n = o(1)$.
    \end{enumerate}
<!-- end lemma-1 -->
<!-- theorem-1: body_md -->
{thm.clt_2cf} 
Let \Cref{ass.clt_2cf} hold. 
Then, 
$$T_n \leadsto \mathcal{N} {\left( 0, 1 \right)}.$$
<!-- end theorem-1 -->
<!-- theorem-2: body_md -->
{thm.clt_2cf.quantitative}
Let \Cref{ass.clt_2cf} hold with $p \ge 3$.
Suppose, in addition, that
$$
n^{\frac{1}{1-1/p}}\gamma_n=O(1),
\qquad 
n  
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Cov}\IfValueT{r_1, r_2}{_{r_1, r_2}}} 
{\operatorname{Cov}\IfValueT{r_1, r_2}{_{r_1, r_2}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}=O(1).
$$
Then,
$$
d_W{\left( T_n, \mathcal{N}{\left( 0,1 \right)} \right)}=O(n^{-1/2}).
$$
<!-- end theorem-2 -->
<!-- remark-1: body_md -->
{remark.stability}
Previous stability-based CLTs for cross-validation require first-order loss stability at the $o(n^{-1})$ scale relative to the variance of the leading score
\citep{bayleCrossvalidationConfidenceIntervals2020,austernAsymptoticsCrossvalidation2025,leiModernTheoryCrossValidation2025}.
Applied to the centered and conditionally normalized score used here, this condition is equivalent to
\[
n\gamma_n\to0.
\]
Indeed, if $\widetilde\sigma_n^2=\operatorname{Var}[\mathbb{E}[f_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}(W)\mid W]]$, their condition is
$n\gamma_n/\widetilde\sigma_n^2\to0$, while
\[
0\le 1-\widetilde\sigma_n^2\le \frac n4\gamma_n.
\]
Hence, previous results cover the regime $n\gamma_n=o(1)$, which also forces $\rho_n\to0$.
By contrast, \Cref{thm.clt_2cf} allows weaker stability, for example $n\gamma_n=O(1)$ when the locality condition makes the covariance contributions asymptotically uncorrelated.
<!-- end remark-1 -->
<!-- theorem-3: body_md -->
{thm.inference}
Let \Cref{ass.clt_2cf} hold after centering and normalizing the scores. 
Suppose $0 < \sigma_\eta^2 < \infty$ for every $\eta\in\mathcal{H}$.
If, for some $\rho > - 1$, 
\begin{equation}
 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{Z_{1,1}Z_{2,1}\mid\mathcal{D}}{_{Z_{1,1}Z_{2,1}\mid\mathcal{D}}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{Z_{1,1}Z_{2,1}\mid\mathcal{D}}{_{Z_{1,1}Z_{2,1}\mid\mathcal{D}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}}{_{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{Z_{1,1}Z_{2,1}\mid\mathcal{D}}{_{Z_{1,1}Z_{2,1}\mid\mathcal{D}}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{Z_{1,1}Z_{2,1}\mid\mathcal{D}}{_{Z_{1,1}Z_{2,1}\mid\mathcal{D}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}}}} 
{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{Z_{1,1}Z_{2,1}\mid\mathcal{D}}{_{Z_{1,1}Z_{2,1}\mid\mathcal{D}}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{Z_{1,1}Z_{2,1}\mid\mathcal{D}}{_{Z_{1,1}Z_{2,1}\mid\mathcal{D}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}}{_{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{Z_{1,1}Z_{2,1}\mid\mathcal{D}}{_{Z_{1,1}Z_{2,1}\mid\mathcal{D}}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{Z_{1,1}Z_{2,1}\mid\mathcal{D}}{_{Z_{1,1}Z_{2,1}\mid\mathcal{D}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}\to0,
\qquad
\rho_{n} \to \rho,
\qquad
B\to\infty,
\label{eq.inference.resampling}
\end{equation}
then $\widehat{\rho}_n-\rho_n\xrightarrow{L^{2}}0$.
If, additionally,
\begin{equation}
\frac{\sigma_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}}{\sigma_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}}\overset{p}{\to}1,
\label{eq.inference.scale_balance}
\end{equation}
then, for every fixed $\alpha\in(0,1)$,
$$
 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{P}}\IfValueT{\theta_{\hat{\eta}}\in\widehat{\mathrm{CI}}_{\alpha}}{_{\theta_{\hat{\eta}}\in\widehat{\mathrm{CI}}_{\alpha}}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{P}}\IfValueT{\theta_{\hat{\eta}}\in\widehat{\mathrm{CI}}_{\alpha}}{_{\theta_{\hat{\eta}}\in\widehat{\mathrm{CI}}_{\alpha}}}\mathopen{}{\left( #2 \right)}}\to1-\alpha.
$$
<!-- end theorem-3 -->
<!-- theorem-4: body_md -->
{thm.clt_K}
Suppose that there exist $\bar c<\infty$, $\underaccent{\bar}{c}>0$, and $p\in(2,\infty]$, with $1/p=0$ when $p=\infty$, such that the first two conditions below hold for all sufficiently large $n$ and the last three hold as $n\to\infty$:
\begin{align}
\left\lVert \tilde f_{{\hat{\eta}}_{-1}}(W) \right\rVert_p
&\le \bar c,
\label{ass.clt_K.moments}\\
V_n
&\ge \underaccent{\bar}{c},
\label{ass.clt_K.degeneracy}\\
\frac{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{\bar v_n}{_{\bar v_n}}} 
{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{\bar v_n}{_{\bar v_n}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}}{V_n^2}
&\to0,
\label{ass.clt_K.scale}\\
n^{\frac{1}{2(1-1/p)}}(K_n-1)\gamma_n
&\to0,
\label{ass.clt_K.stability}\\
\frac{(K_n-1)^2 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{\bar r_n}{_{\bar r_n}}} 
{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{\bar r_n}{_{\bar r_n}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}}{V_n^2}
&\to0.
\label{ass.clt_K.locality}
\end{align}
Then
$$
T_n\leadsto\mathcal{N}{\left( 0,1 \right)}.
$$
<!-- end theorem-4 -->
<!-- proof-1: body_md -->
For $i=1,\dots,n$, write $f_i=\tilde f_{{\hat{\eta}}_{-k(i)}}(W_i)$, so that $T_n=(nV_n)^{-1/2}\sum_{i=1}^n f_i$.
Since ${\hat{\eta}}_{-k(i)}$ is $\mathcal{D}_{-i}$-measurable, \cref{eq.clt_K.normalization,eq.clt_K.v} give
\begin{equation}
 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{f_i\mid\mathcal{D}_{-i}}{_{f_i\mid\mathcal{D}_{-i}}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{f_i\mid\mathcal{D}_{-i}}{_{f_i\mid\mathcal{D}_{-i}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}=0,
\qquad
 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{f_i^2\mid\mathcal{D}_{-i}}{_{f_i^2\mid\mathcal{D}_{-i}}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{f_i^2\mid\mathcal{D}_{-i}}{_{f_i^2\mid\mathcal{D}_{-i}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}=v_{k(i),n}.
\label{eq.clt_K.proof.conditional}
\end{equation}
When $p<\infty$, the conditional Jensen inequality gives $v_{k,n}^{p/2}\le 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\left\lvert \tilde f_{{\hat{\eta}}_{-k}}(W) \right\rvert^p\mid{\hat{\eta}}_{-k}}{_{\left\lvert \tilde f_{{\hat{\eta}}_{-k}}(W) \right\rvert^p\mid{\hat{\eta}}_{-k}}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\left\lvert \tilde f_{{\hat{\eta}}_{-k}}(W) \right\rvert^p\mid{\hat{\eta}}_{-k}}{_{\left\lvert \tilde f_{{\hat{\eta}}_{-k}}(W) \right\rvert^p\mid{\hat{\eta}}_{-k}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}$, and when $p=\infty$, $v_{k,n}\le\bar c^2$ directly.
Hence \cref{ass.clt_K.moments} and fold symmetry give, for all $i$ and $k$,
\begin{equation}
\left\lVert f_i \right\rVert_p\le\bar c,
\qquad
\left\lVert v_{k,n}^{1/2} \right\rVert_p\le\bar c.
\label{eq.clt_K.proof.moments}
\end{equation}
For $i=1,\dots,n$, let $\Delta_i=T_n- 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{T_n\mid\mathcal{D}_{-i}}{_{T_n\mid\mathcal{D}_{-i}}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{T_n\mid\mathcal{D}_{-i}}{_{T_n\mid\mathcal{D}_{-i}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}$ and, for $\ell\ne k(i)$, let $A_{i,\ell}=S_{\ell,n}- 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{S_{\ell,n}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-i}}{_{S_{\ell,n}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-i}}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{S_{\ell,n}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-i}}{_{S_{\ell,n}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-i}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}$.
Let $q=1/(1-1/p)\in[1,2)$ denote the conjugate exponent of $p$.

\paragraph{Step 1. Increment bound.}
Conditional on $\mathcal{D}_{-i}$, the scores $f_j$, $j\in\mathsf{s}_{k(i)}\setminus\left\lbrace i \right\rbrace$, are fixed, while $f_i$ and the models ${\hat{\eta}}_{-\ell}$, $\ell\ne k(i)$, vary.
Hence, by \cref{eq.clt_K.proof.conditional},
\begin{equation}
\Delta_i
=
\frac{f_i}{\sqrt{nV_n}}
+
\frac{1}{\sqrt{K_nV_n}}
\sum_{\ell\ne k(i)}A_{i,\ell}.
\label{eq.clt_K.proof.delta}
\end{equation}
Let ${\hat{\eta}}_{-\ell}'$ denote the model obtained after replacing $W_i$ by an independent copy in the training sample of ${\hat{\eta}}_{-\ell}$, and let $S_{\ell,n}'$ denote $S_{\ell,n}$ computed with ${\hat{\eta}}_{-\ell}'$ in place of ${\hat{\eta}}_{-\ell}$.
Conditional on $\mathcal{D}_{-i}$, $S_{\ell,n}$ and $S_{\ell,n}'$ are independent and identically distributed, so $ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{A_{i,\ell}^2}{_{A_{i,\ell}^2}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{A_{i,\ell}^2}{_{A_{i,\ell}^2}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}=\frac12 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\left( S_{\ell,n}-S_{\ell,n}' \right)^2}{_{\left( S_{\ell,n}-S_{\ell,n}' \right)^2}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\left( S_{\ell,n}-S_{\ell,n}' \right)^2}{_{\left( S_{\ell,n}-S_{\ell,n}' \right)^2}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}$.
The observations in $\mathsf{s}_\ell$ are independent of $({\hat{\eta}}_{-\ell},{\hat{\eta}}_{-\ell}')$, so, conditional on these models, the differences $\tilde f_{{\hat{\eta}}_{-\ell}}(W_j)-\tilde f_{{\hat{\eta}}_{-\ell}'}(W_j)$, $j\in\mathsf{s}_\ell$, are i.i.d.\ with mean zero, since $P\tilde f_\eta=0$ for every $\eta\in\mathcal{H}$.
Hence the cross terms of the square vanish, and fold symmetry and exchangeability within folds give $ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\left( S_{\ell,n}-S_{\ell,n}' \right)^2}{_{\left( S_{\ell,n}-S_{\ell,n}' \right)^2}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\left( S_{\ell,n}-S_{\ell,n}' \right)^2}{_{\left( S_{\ell,n}-S_{\ell,n}' \right)^2}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}=\gamma_n$, so $ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{A_{i,\ell}^2}{_{A_{i,\ell}^2}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{A_{i,\ell}^2}{_{A_{i,\ell}^2}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}=\gamma_n/2$.
By \cref{eq.clt_K.proof.delta}, the Cauchy--Schwarz inequality, $ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{f_i^2}{_{f_i^2}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{f_i^2}{_{f_i^2}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}=1$, $(K_n-1)^2/K_n\le K_n-1$, and \cref{ass.clt_K.degeneracy},
\begin{equation}
 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\Delta_i^2}{_{\Delta_i^2}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\Delta_i^2}{_{\Delta_i^2}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}
\le
\frac{2}{nV_n}
+
\frac{(K_n-1)^2}{K_nV_n}\gamma_n
\le
\frac{2}{\underaccent{\bar}{c}}
\left\lbrace \frac1n+(K_n-1)\gamma_n \right\rbrace.
\label{eq.clt_K.proof.delta_bound}
\end{equation}

\paragraph{Step 2. Kernel concentration.}
For $i=1,\dots,n$, let
$$
c_i
=
 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Cov}\IfValueT{f_i,\sum_{j=1}^n f_j\,\middle|\,\mathcal{D}_{-i}}{_{f_i,\sum_{j=1}^n f_j\,\middle|\,\mathcal{D}_{-i}}}} 
{\operatorname{Cov}\IfValueT{f_i,\sum_{j=1}^n f_j\,\middle|\,\mathcal{D}_{-i}}{_{f_i,\sum_{j=1}^n f_j\,\middle|\,\mathcal{D}_{-i}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}
=
\sqrt{nV_n}\, 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{f_i\Delta_i\mid\mathcal{D}_{-i}}{_{f_i\Delta_i\mid\mathcal{D}_{-i}}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{f_i\Delta_i\mid\mathcal{D}_{-i}}{_{f_i\Delta_i\mid\mathcal{D}_{-i}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]},
$$
where the second equality uses $ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{f_i\mid\mathcal{D}_{-i}}{_{f_i\mid\mathcal{D}_{-i}}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{f_i\mid\mathcal{D}_{-i}}{_{f_i\mid\mathcal{D}_{-i}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}=0$.
The term $j=i$ contributes $v_{k(i),n}$ by \cref{eq.clt_K.proof.conditional}, the terms $j\in\mathsf{s}_{k(i)}\setminus\left\lbrace i \right\rbrace$ are $\mathcal{D}_{-i}$-measurable and contribute zero, and $\sum_{j\in\mathsf{s}_\ell}f_j=\sqrt{m_n}\,S_{\ell,n}$ for $\ell\ne k(i)$, so \cref{eq.clt_K.r} gives
\begin{equation}
c_i=v_{k(i),n}+(K_n-1)r_i.
\label{eq.clt_K.proof.c}
\end{equation}
Since $\sum_{i=1}^n v_{k(i),n}=m_n\sum_{k=1}^{K_n}v_{k,n}$, \cref{eq.clt_K.kernel} yields $\mathcal{K}_n=(nV_n)^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^n c_i$.
Since $ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\mathcal{K}_n}{_{\mathcal{K}_n}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\mathcal{K}_n}{_{\mathcal{K}_n}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}=1$ and $ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{X+Y}{_{X+Y}}} 
{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{X+Y}{_{X+Y}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}\le2 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{X}{_{X}}} 
{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{X}{_{X}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}+2 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{Y}{_{Y}}} 
{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{Y}{_{Y}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}$,
\begin{equation}
 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\left\lvert \mathcal{K}_n-1 \right\rvert}{_{\left\lvert \mathcal{K}_n-1 \right\rvert}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\left\lvert \mathcal{K}_n-1 \right\rvert}{_{\left\lvert \mathcal{K}_n-1 \right\rvert}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}^2
\le
 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{\mathcal{K}_n}{_{\mathcal{K}_n}}} 
{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{\mathcal{K}_n}{_{\mathcal{K}_n}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}
\le
\frac{2 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{\bar v_n}{_{\bar v_n}}} 
{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{\bar v_n}{_{\bar v_n}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}}{V_n^2}
+
\frac{2(K_n-1)^2 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{\bar r_n}{_{\bar r_n}}} 
{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{\bar r_n}{_{\bar r_n}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}}{V_n^2},
\label{eq.clt_K.proof.kernel}
\end{equation}
which converges to zero by \cref{ass.clt_K.scale,ass.clt_K.locality}.

\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\Delta_i^2}{_{\Delta_i^2}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\Delta_i^2}{_{\Delta_i^2}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}^{1-1/p}
\le
C\sqrt n\left\lbrace \frac1n+(K_n-1)\gamma_n \right\rbrace^{1-1/p},
\label{eq.clt_K.proof.stein}
\end{equation}
where $C=4\bar c\,\underaccent{\bar}{c}^{-1/2}(2/\underaccent{\bar}{c})^{1-1/p}$.

\paragraph{Step 4. Conclusion.}
By \cref{eq.clt_K.proof.reduction,eq.clt_K.proof.kernel,eq.clt_K.proof.stein}, it remains to show that the right-hand side of \cref{eq.clt_K.proof.stein} vanishes.
Since $(a+b)^s\le a^s+b^s$ for $a,b\ge0$ and $s\in(0,1]$,
$$
\sqrt n\left\lbrace \frac1n+(K_n-1)\gamma_n \right\rbrace^{1-1/p}
\le
n^{-\left( \frac12-\frac1p \right)}
+
\left\lbrace n^{\frac{1}{2(1-1/p)}}(K_n-1)\gamma_n \right\rbrace^{1-1/p},
$$
which converges to zero because $p>2$ and by \cref{ass.clt_K.stability}.
Hence $d_W{\left( T_n,\mathcal{N}{\left( 0,1 \right)} \right)}\to0$, which implies $T_n\leadsto\mathcal{N}{\left( 0,1 \right)}$.
<!-- end proof-1 -->

{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} \,\middle|\, \mathcal{D}_{-1}}{_{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} \,\middle|\, \mathcal{D}_{-1}}}} 
{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} \,\middle|\, \mathcal{D}_{-1}}{_{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} \,\middle|\, \mathcal{D}_{-1}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}}{_{

{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} \,\middle|\, \mathcal{D}_{-1}}{_{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} \,\middle|\, \mathcal{D}_{-1}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]} = \frac{\gamma_n}{n},
    \end{equation}
    and 
    \begin{equation} \label{lem.clt_2cf.stability_bounds.r}
         
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{r_1}{_{r_1}}} 
{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{r_1}{_{r_1}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]} \le

{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{r_1^2}{_{r_1^2}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]} \le \frac{n \gamma_n}{4}.

}{_{

}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{

}{_{

{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{

}{_{

&=
\frac12
 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\left( \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}-\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}'_1} \right)^2}{_{\left( \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}-\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}'_1} \right)^2}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\left( \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}-\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}'_1} \right)^2}{_{\left( \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}-\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}'_1} \right)^2}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}
\nonumber\\
&=
\frac{2}{n^2}
 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{
\left( 
\sum_{j\in\mathsf{s}_2}
\left\lbrace f_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}(W_j)-f_{{\hat{\eta}}'_1}(W_j) \right\rbrace
 \right)^2
}{_{
\left( 
\sum_{j\in\mathsf{s}_2}
\left\lbrace f_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}(W_j)-f_{{\hat{\eta}}'_1}(W_j) \right\rbrace
 \right)^2
}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{
\left( 
\sum_{j\in\mathsf{s}_2}
\left\lbrace f_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}(W_j)-f_{{\hat{\eta}}'_1}(W_j) \right\rbrace
 \right)^2
}{_{
\left( 
\sum_{j\in\mathsf{s}_2}
\left\lbrace f_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}(W_j)-f_{{\hat{\eta}}'_1}(W_j) \right\rbrace
 \right)^2
}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}
\nonumber\\
&=
\frac{2}{n^2}
\sum_{j\in\mathsf{s}_2}
 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\left( f_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}(W_j)-f_{{\hat{\eta}}'_1}(W_j) \right)^2}{_{\left( f_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}(W_j)-f_{{\hat{\eta}}'_1}(W_j) \right)^2}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\left( f_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}(W_j)-f_{{\hat{\eta}}'_1}(W_j) \right)^2}{_{\left( f_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}(W_j)-f_{{\hat{\eta}}'_1}(W_j) \right)^2}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}
\label{lem.clt_2cf.stability_bounds.evaluation}\\
&=
\frac{2}{n^2}\cdot\frac n2\,\gamma_n
=
\frac{\gamma_n}{n}.
\nonumber
\end{align}
\cref{lem.clt_2cf.stability_bounds.replacement} uses that, conditional on $\mathcal{D}_{-1}$, $\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}$ and $\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}'_1}$ are independent and identically distributed, so that $ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{X \mid \mathcal{D}_{-1}}{_{X \mid \mathcal{D}_{-1}}}} 
{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{X \mid \mathcal{D}_{-1}}{_{X \mid \mathcal{D}_{-1}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]} = \frac12  
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{(X - X')^2 \mid \mathcal{D}_{-1}}{_{(X - X')^2 \mid \mathcal{D}_{-1}}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{(X - X')^2 \mid \mathcal{D}_{-1}}{_{(X - X')^2 \mid \mathcal{D}_{-1}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}$.
\cref{lem.clt_2cf.stability_bounds.evaluation} expands the square; the cross terms vanish because, conditional on $({\hat{\eta}}_1, {\hat{\eta}}'_1)$, the differences $f_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}(W_j)-f_{{\hat{\eta}}'_1}(W_j)$ are independent across $j \in \mathsf{s}_2$ with mean $P f_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} - P f_{{\hat{\eta}}'_1} = 0$.
The last equality uses that $W_j$, $j \in \mathsf{s}_2$, is independent of $({\hat{\eta}}_1, {\hat{\eta}}'_1)$ and $\left\lvert \mathsf{s}_2 \right\rvert = n/2$.

For \cref{lem.clt_2cf.stability_bounds.r}, the first inequality is $ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{X}{_{X}}} 
{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{X}{_{X}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]} \le  
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{X^2}{_{X^2}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{X^2}{_{X^2}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}$, and
\begin{align}
 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{r_1^2}{_{r_1^2}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{r_1^2}{_{r_1^2}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}
&\le
\frac{n^2}{4} 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{
 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{\sqrt{n/2}\,\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}{_{\sqrt{n/2}\,\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}}} 
{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{\sqrt{n/2}\,\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}{_{\sqrt{n/2}\,\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}
 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{\sqrt{n/2}\,\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}{_{\sqrt{n/2}\,\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}}} 
{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{\sqrt{n/2}\,\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}{_{\sqrt{n/2}\,\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}
}{_{
 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{\sqrt{n/2}\,\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}{_{\sqrt{n/2}\,\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}}} 
{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{\sqrt{n/2}\,\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}{_{\sqrt{n/2}\,\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}
 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{\sqrt{n/2}\,\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}{_{\sqrt{n/2}\,\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}}} 
{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{\sqrt{n/2}\,\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}{_{\sqrt{n/2}\,\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}
}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{

}{_{

\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{\sqrt{n/2}\,\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}{_{\sqrt{n/2}\,\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}}} 
{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{\sqrt{n/2}\,\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}{_{\sqrt{n/2}\,\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}
}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}
\label{lem.clt_2cf.stability_bounds.cs}\\
&=
\frac{n^2}{4} 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}{_{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}}} 
{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}{_{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}}{_{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}{_{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}}} 
{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}{_{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}{_{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}}} 
{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}{_{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}}{_{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}{_{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}}} 
{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}{_{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}
\label{lem.clt_2cf.stability_bounds.cond_var}\\
&=
\frac{n\gamma_n}{4}.
\nonumber
\end{align}
\cref{lem.clt_2cf.stability_bounds.cs} is conditional Cauchy--Schwarz.
\cref{lem.clt_2cf.stability_bounds.cond_var} uses that ${\hat{\eta}}_2$ and $W_i$, $i \in \mathsf{s}_1 \setminus \left\lbrace 1 \right\rbrace$, are $\mathcal{D}_{-1}$-measurable, so that only $f_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}(W_1)$ varies and, by the standardization,
$$
 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{\sqrt{n/2}\,\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}{_{\sqrt{n/2}\,\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}}} 
{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{\sqrt{n/2}\,\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}{_{\sqrt{n/2}\,\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}
=
\frac n2\left( \frac2n \right)^2 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{f_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}(W_1)\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}{_{f_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}(W_1)\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}}} 
{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{f_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}(W_1)\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}{_{f_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}(W_1)\mid\mathcal{D}_{-1}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}
=
\frac2n.
$$
The last equality is \cref{lem.clt_2cf.stability_bounds.variance}.

For \cref{lem.clt_2cf.stability_bounds.rho}, 
\begin{align}
     
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{r_1}{_{r_1}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{r_1}{_{r_1}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]} & = \frac{n^2}{4}  
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Cov}\IfValueT{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}, \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_2} \;\middle|\; \mathcal{D}_{-1} }{_{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}, \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_2} \;\middle|\; \mathcal{D}_{-1} }}} 
{\operatorname{Cov}\IfValueT{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}, \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_2} \;\middle|\; \mathcal{D}_{-1} }{_{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}, \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_2} \;\middle|\; \mathcal{D}_{-1} }}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}}{_{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Cov}\IfValueT{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}, \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_2} \;\middle|\; \mathcal{D}_{-1} }{_{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}, \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_2} \;\middle|\; \mathcal{D}_{-1} }}} 
{\operatorname{Cov}\IfValueT{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}, \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_2} \;\middle|\; \mathcal{D}_{-1} }{_{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}, \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_2} \;\middle|\; \mathcal{D}_{-1} }}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Cov}\IfValueT{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}, \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_2} \;\middle|\; \mathcal{D}_{-1} }{_{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}, \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_2} \;\middle|\; \mathcal{D}_{-1} }}} 
{\operatorname{Cov}\IfValueT{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}, \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_2} \;\middle|\; \mathcal{D}_{-1} }{_{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}, \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_2} \;\middle|\; \mathcal{D}_{-1} }}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}}{_{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Cov}\IfValueT{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}, \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_2} \;\middle|\; \mathcal{D}_{-1} }{_{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}, \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_2} \;\middle|\; \mathcal{D}_{-1} }}} 
{\operatorname{Cov}\IfValueT{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}, \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_2} \;\middle|\; \mathcal{D}_{-1} }{_{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}, \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_2} \;\middle|\; \mathcal{D}_{-1} }}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]} \nonumber \\
    & = \frac{n}{2}  
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Cov}\IfValueT{f_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}(W_1), \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} \;\middle|\; \mathcal{D}_{-1} }{_{f_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}(W_1), \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} \;\middle|\; \mathcal{D}_{-1} }}} 
{\operatorname{Cov}\IfValueT{f_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}(W_1), \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} \;\middle|\; \mathcal{D}_{-1} }{_{f_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}(W_1), \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} \;\middle|\; \mathcal{D}_{-1} }}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}}{_{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Cov}\IfValueT{f_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}(W_1), \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} \;\middle|\; \mathcal{D}_{-1} }{_{f_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}(W_1), \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} \;\middle|\; \mathcal{D}_{-1} }}} 
{\operatorname{Cov}\IfValueT{f_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}(W_1), \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} \;\middle|\; \mathcal{D}_{-1} }{_{f_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}(W_1), \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} \;\middle|\; \mathcal{D}_{-1} }}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Cov}\IfValueT{f_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}(W_1), \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} \;\middle|\; \mathcal{D}_{-1} }{_{f_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}(W_1), \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} \;\middle|\; \mathcal{D}_{-1} }}} 
{\operatorname{Cov}\IfValueT{f_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}(W_1), \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} \;\middle|\; \mathcal{D}_{-1} }{_{f_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}(W_1), \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} \;\middle|\; \mathcal{D}_{-1} }}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}}{_{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Cov}\IfValueT{f_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}(W_1), \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} \;\middle|\; \mathcal{D}_{-1} }{_{f_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}(W_1), \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} \;\middle|\; \mathcal{D}_{-1} }}} 
{\operatorname{Cov}\IfValueT{f_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}(W_1), \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} \;\middle|\; \mathcal{D}_{-1} }{_{f_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}(W_1), \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} \;\middle|\; \mathcal{D}_{-1} }}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]} \nonumber \\
    & = \frac{n}{2}  
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{f_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}(W_1) \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} }{_{f_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}(W_1) \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} }}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{f_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}(W_1) \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} }{_{f_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}(W_1) \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} }}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]} \label{lem.clt_2cf.stability_bounds.cross_moment} \\
    & = \frac{n}{2}  
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Cov}\IfValueT{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}, \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_2} }{_{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}, \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_2} }}} 
{\operatorname{Cov}\IfValueT{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}, \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_2} }{_{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}, \hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_2} }}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]} \nonumber \\
    & = \rho_n. \nonumber 
\end{align}
The second equality uses that ${\hat{\eta}}_2$ and $W_i$, $i \in \mathsf{s}_1 \setminus \left\lbrace 1 \right\rbrace$, are $\mathcal{D}_{-1}$-measurable. 
\cref{lem.clt_2cf.stability_bounds.cross_moment} uses $ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{f_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}(W_1) \mid \mathcal{D}_{-1}}{_{f_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}(W_1) \mid \mathcal{D}_{-1}}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{f_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}(W_1) \mid \mathcal{D}_{-1}}{_{f_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}(W_1) \mid \mathcal{D}_{-1}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]} = P f_{{\hat{\eta}}_2} = 0$. 
The fourth equality uses exchangeability within $\mathsf{s}_1$ and $ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}}{_{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}}{_{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_2}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]} = 0$, and the last uses $ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_k}}{_{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_k}}}} 
{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_k}}{_{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_k}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]} = 2/n$, which follows from the standardization. 
Finally, by Jensen's inequality and \cref{lem.clt_2cf.stability_bounds.r}, $\left\lvert \rho_n \right\rvert = \left\lvert  
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{r_1}{_{r_1}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{r_1}{_{r_1}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]} \right\rvert \le \sqrt{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{r_1^2}{_{r_1^2}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{r_1^2}{_{r_1^2}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}} \le \frac{1}{2} \sqrt{n \gamma_n}$.
<!-- end proof-2 -->
<!-- proof-3: body_md -->
By exchangeability within $\mathsf{s}_1$, $ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{r_1}{_{r_1}}} 
{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{r_1}{_{r_1}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]} =  
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{r_2}{_{r_2}}} 
{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{r_2}{_{r_2}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}$, so $ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Cov}\IfValueT{r_1, r_2}{_{r_1, r_2}}} 
{\operatorname{Cov}\IfValueT{r_1, r_2}{_{r_1, r_2}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]} =  
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{r_1}{_{r_1}}} 
{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{r_1}{_{r_1}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}  
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Corr}\IfValueT{r_1, r_2}{_{r_1, r_2}}} 
{\operatorname{Corr}\IfValueT{r_1, r_2}{_{r_1, r_2}}\mathopen{}{\left( #2 \right)}}$, where both sides are zero if $ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{r_1}{_{r_1}}} 
{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{r_1}{_{r_1}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]} = 0$.
Hence, by \cref{lem.clt_2cf.stability_bounds.r} and $\left\lvert  
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Corr}\IfValueT{r_1, r_2}{_{r_1, r_2}}} 
{\operatorname{Corr}\IfValueT{r_1, r_2}{_{r_1, r_2}}\mathopen{}{\left( #2 \right)}} \right\rvert \le 1$,
$$\left\lvert  
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Cov}\IfValueT{r_1, r_2}{_{r_1, r_2}}} 
{\operatorname{Cov}\IfValueT{r_1, r_2}{_{r_1, r_2}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]} \right\rvert \le \frac{n \gamma_n}{4} \left\lvert  
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Corr}\IfValueT{r_1, r_2}{_{r_1, r_2}}} 
{\operatorname{Corr}\IfValueT{r_1, r_2}{_{r_1, r_2}}\mathopen{}{\left( #2 \right)}} \right\rvert \le \frac{n \gamma_n}{4}.$$
Under (a), $n^{\frac{1}{2(1-1/p)}} \gamma_n = n^{-\frac{1-2/p}{2(1-1/p)}} \cdot n \gamma_n \to 0$ since $p > 2$, and the middle term is $O(1) \cdot o(1)$.
Under (b), the first condition is \Cref{ass.clt_2cf.stability} with $p = \infty$, and the middle term equals $\frac{1}{4} \sqrt{n} \gamma_n \cdot \sqrt{n} \left\lvert  
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Corr}\IfValueT{r_1, r_2}{_{r_1, r_2}}} 
{\operatorname{Corr}\IfValueT{r_1, r_2}{_{r_1, r_2}}\mathopen{}{\left( #2 \right)}} \right\rvert = o(1) \cdot O(1)$.
Under (c), $n^{\frac{1}{2(1-1/p)}} \gamma_n \le n \gamma_n \to 0$ since $\frac{1}{2(1-1/p)} \le 1$, and the right-hand side is $o(1)$.
<!-- end proof-3 -->

<!-- proof-4: body_md -->
The proof uses Stein's method of normal approximation \citep{steinBoundErrorNormal1972,steinApproximateComputationExpectations1986}. 
Its starting point is that a random variable $Z$ follows a standard normal distribution, $Z \sim \mathcal{N} {\left( 0,1 \right)}$, if, and only if,
$$ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{Z \varphi(Z)}{_{Z \varphi(Z)}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{Z \varphi(Z)}{_{Z \varphi(Z)}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]} =  
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\varphi'(Z)}{_{\varphi'(Z)}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\varphi'(Z)}{_{\varphi'(Z)}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}$$
for every $\varphi$ in a sufficiently rich class. 
This characterization suggests that if there is a random variable $\mathcal{K}_n$, often called an approximate Stein kernel, satisfying two properties, 
$$ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{T_n \varphi(T_n)}{_{T_n \varphi(T_n)}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{T_n \varphi(T_n)}{_{T_n \varphi(T_n)}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]} -  
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\mathcal{K}_n \varphi'(T_n)}{_{\mathcal{K}_n \varphi'(T_n)}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\mathcal{K}_n \varphi'(T_n)}{_{\mathcal{K}_n \varphi'(T_n)}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]} \approx 0,$$
and 
$$\mathcal{K}_n \xrightarrow{L^{1}} 1,$$
then $T_n$ converges to a normal distribution. 
The main novelty of this proof is in constructing a specific $\mathcal{K}_n$ that satisfies both requirements under the locality and regularity conditions. 

First, for any $h \in  
\IfNoValueTF{#1}{\operatorname{Lip}} 
{\operatorname{Lip}\mathopen{}{\left( #1 \right)}}{1}$, let $\varphi_h$ be the solution to Stein's equation 
\begin{equation} \label{eq.stein_eq}
    h(t) -  
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{h(Z)}{_{h(Z)}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{h(Z)}{_{h(Z)}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]} = \varphi'_h(t) - t \varphi_h(t), \qquad t \in \mathbb{R},
\end{equation}
given by 
$$\varphi_h(t) = e^{t^2/2} \int_{-\infty}^t \left[ h(x) -  
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{h(Z)}{_{h(Z)}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{h(Z)}{_{h(Z)}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]} \right] e^{-x^2/2} \, \mathrm{d} x.$$
In particular, 
$\left\lVert \varphi_h' \right\rVert_\infty \le 1$ and $\lvert \varphi_h'(x) - \varphi_h'(y) \rvert \le 2 \lvert x - y \rvert$ for all $x, y \in \mathbb{R}$ \citep[Lemma 4.2]{chatterjeeNewMethodNormal2008a}.
Second, define the Stein remainder
\begin{equation} \label{eq.remainder}
    R_n(\varphi_h) =  
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{T_n \varphi_h (T_n)}{_{T_n \varphi_h (T_n)}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{T_n \varphi_h (T_n)}{_{T_n \varphi_h (T_n)}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]} -  
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\mathcal{K}_n \varphi_h'(T_n)}{_{\mathcal{K}_n \varphi_h'(T_n)}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\mathcal{K}_n \varphi_h'(T_n)}{_{\mathcal{K}_n \varphi_h'(T_n)}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}.
\end{equation} 
Combining \cref{eq.stein_eq}, \cref{eq.remainder}, and $\left\lVert \varphi_h' \right\rVert_\infty \le 1$ yields the inequality 
\begin{align*}
    \left\lvert  
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{h(T_n)}{_{h(T_n)}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{h(T_n)}{_{h(T_n)}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]} -  
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{h(Z)}{_{h(Z)}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{h(Z)}{_{h(Z)}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]} \right\rvert & = \left\lvert  
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{T_n \varphi_h(T_n)}{_{T_n \varphi_h(T_n)}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{T_n \varphi_h(T_n)}{_{T_n \varphi_h(T_n)}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]} -  
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\varphi'_h(T_n)}{_{\varphi'_h(T_n)}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\varphi'_h(T_n)}{_{\varphi'_h(T_n)}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]} \right\rvert \\  
    & = \left\lvert  
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{(1-\mathcal{K}_n) \varphi'_h(T_n)}{_{(1-\mathcal{K}_n) \varphi'_h(T_n)}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{(1-\mathcal{K}_n) \varphi'_h(T_n)}{_{(1-\mathcal{K}_n) \varphi'_h(T_n)}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]} - R_n(\varphi_h) \right\rvert \\
    & \le  
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\left\lvert \mathcal{K}_n - 1 \right\rvert}{_{\left\lvert \mathcal{K}_n - 1 \right\rvert}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\left\lvert \mathcal{K}_n - 1 \right\rvert}{_{\left\lvert \mathcal{K}_n - 1 \right\rvert}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]} + \left\lvert R_n(\varphi_h) \right\rvert.
\end{align*}
Hence, 
\begin{equation} \label{thm.clt_2cf.stein_bound.reduction}
    d_W{\left( T_n, \mathcal{N}{\left( 0,1 \right)} \right)} \le  
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\left\lvert \mathcal{K}_n - 1 \right\rvert}{_{\left\lvert \mathcal{K}_n - 1 \right\rvert}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\left\lvert \mathcal{K}_n - 1 \right\rvert}{_{\left\lvert \mathcal{K}_n - 1 \right\rvert}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]} +  
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\sup\IfValueT{\IfValueT}{_{\IfValueT}}} 
{\sup\IfValueT{\IfValueT}{_{\IfValueT}}\mathopen{}{\left( #2 \right)}}{\IfValueT}{_{\IfValueT}}} 
{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\sup\IfValueT{\IfValueT}{_{\IfValueT}}}

The proof proceeds in two steps. 
Step 1 establishes \cref{thm.clt_2cf.stein_bound.mean,thm.clt_2cf.stein_bound.kernel} and bounds the first term in \cref{thm.clt_2cf.stein_bound.reduction}. 
Step 2 bounds the second term and establishes \cref{thm.clt_2cf.stein_bound.increment}. 

\paragraph{Step 1. Kernel Concentration.}

\begin{align}
     
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\left\lvert \mathcal{K}_n - 1 \right\rvert}{_{\left\lvert \mathcal{K}_n - 1 \right\rvert}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\left\lvert \mathcal{K}_n - 1 \right\rvert}{_{\left\lvert \mathcal{K}_n - 1 \right\rvert}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}^2 & \le

\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{r_1}{_{r_1}}} 
{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{r_1}{_{r_1}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}}{n} + \frac{1}{(1 + \rho_n)^2} \left( 1 - \frac{2}{n} \right)

{\operatorname{Cov}\IfValueT{r_1, r_2}{_{r_1, r_2}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}. \nonumber

\cref{thm.clt_2cf.step1.1} follows since 
$$

by exchangeability of observations within $\mathsf{s}_1$ and \cref{lem.clt_2cf.stability_bounds.rho}.
\cref{thm.clt_2cf.step1.2} uses exchangeability within $\mathsf{s}_1$. 
This proves \cref{thm.clt_2cf.stein_bound.mean,thm.clt_2cf.stein_bound.kernel}.

{\max\IfValueT{\IfValueT}{_{\IfValueT}}\mathopen{}{\left( #2 \right)}}{\IfValueT}{_{\IfValueT}}\mathopen{}{\left( #2 \right)}}{\IfValueT}{_{\IfValueT}}} 
{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\max\IfValueT{\IfValueT}{_{\IfValueT}}} 
{\max\IfValueT{\IfValueT}{_{\IfValueT}}\mathopen{}{\left( #2 \right)}}{\IfValueT}{_{\IfValueT}}} 
{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\max\IfValueT{\IfValueT}{_{\IfValueT}}} 
{\max\IfValueT{\IfValueT}{_{\IfValueT}}\mathopen{}{\left( #2 \right)}}{\IfValueT}{_{\IfValueT}}\mathopen{}{\left( #2 \right)}}{\IfValueT}{_{\IfValueT}}\mathopen{}{\left( #2 \right)}}{\{}{_{\{}}\mathopen{}{\left( #2 \right)}}\phi(a), \phi(b)\} \le 4 \phi(a) + 4 \phi(b),
$$
and hence
\begin{equation} \label{thm.clt_2cf.quantitative.split}
     
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\left\lvert f_1 \right\rvert \phi(\Delta_1)}{_{\left\lvert f_1 \right\rvert \phi(\Delta_1)}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\left\lvert f_1 \right\rvert \phi(\Delta_1)}{_{\left\lvert f_1 \right\rvert \phi(\Delta_1)}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]} \le 4  
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\left\lvert f_1 \right\rvert \phi(A)}{_{\left\lvert f_1 \right\rvert \phi(A)}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\left\lvert f_1 \right\rvert \phi(A)}{_{\left\lvert f_1 \right\rvert \phi(A)}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]} + 4  
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\left\lvert f_1 \right\rvert \phi(B)}{_{\left\lvert f_1 \right\rvert \phi(B)}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\left\lvert f_1 \right\rvert \phi(B)}{_{\left\lvert f_1 \right\rvert \phi(B)}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}.
\end{equation}
For the first term in \cref{thm.clt_2cf.quantitative.split}, $\phi(A) \le A^2$ and Lyapunov's inequality with $p \ge 3$ give
$$
 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\left\lvert f_1 \right\rvert \phi(A)}{_{\left\lvert f_1 \right\rvert \phi(A)}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\left\lvert f_1 \right\rvert \phi(A)}{_{\left\lvert f_1 \right\rvert \phi(A)}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}
\le
\frac{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\left\lvert f_1 \right\rvert^3}{_{\left\lvert f_1 \right\rvert^3}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\left\lvert f_1 \right\rvert^3}{_{\left\lvert f_1 \right\rvert^3}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}}{n(1+\rho_n)}
\le
\frac{\bar c^3}{\underaccent{\bar}{c} \, n}.
$$
For the second term, set $\alpha = 1 - 2/p \in [1/3, 1]$ and $q = 1/(1-1/p) \in [1, 3/2]$, so that $(1+\alpha) q = 2$ and $1/q = 1 - 1/p$.
Since $ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\min\IfValueT{\IfValueT}{_{\IfValueT}}} 
{\min\IfValueT{\IfValueT}{_{\IfValueT}}\mathopen{}{\left( #2 \right)}}{\IfValueT}{_{\IfValueT}}} 
{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\min\IfValueT{\IfValueT}{_{\IfValueT}}} 
{\min\IfValueT{\IfValueT}{_{\IfValueT}}\mathopen{}{\left( #2 \right)}}{\IfValueT}{_{\IfValueT}}\mathopen{}{\left( #2 \right)}}{\IfValueT}{_{\IfValueT}}} 
{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\min\IfValueT{\IfValueT}{_{\IfValueT}}} 
{\min\IfValueT{\IfValueT}{_{\IfValueT}}\mathopen{}{\left( #2 \right)}}{\IfValueT}{_{\IfValueT}}} 
{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\min\IfValueT{\IfValueT}{_{\IfValueT}}} 
{\min\IfValueT{\IfValueT}{_{\IfValueT}}\mathopen{}{\left( #2 \right)}}{\IfValueT}{_{\IfValueT}}\mathopen{}{\left( #2 \right)}}{\IfValueT}{_{\IfValueT}}\mathopen{}{\left( #2 \right)}}{\{}{_{\{}}} 
{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\min\IfValueT{\IfValueT}{_{\IfValueT}}} 
{\min\IfValueT{\IfValueT}{_{\IfValueT}}\mathopen{}{\left( #2 \right)}}{\IfValueT}{_{\IfValueT}}} 
{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\min\IfValueT{\IfValueT}{_{\IfValueT}}} 
{\min\IfValueT{\IfValueT}{_{\IfValueT}}\mathopen{}{\left( #2 \right)}}{\IfValueT}{_{\IfValueT}}\mathopen{}{\left( #2 \right)}}{\IfValueT}{_{\IfValueT}}} 
{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\min\IfValueT{\IfValueT}{_{\IfValueT}}} 
{\min\IfValueT{\IfValueT}{_{\IfValueT}}\mathopen{}{\left( #2 \right)}}{\IfValueT}{_{\IfValueT}}} 
{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\min\IfValueT{\IfValueT}{_{\IfValueT}}} 
{\min\IfValueT{\IfValueT}{_{\IfValueT}}\mathopen{}{\left( #2 \right)}}{\IfValueT}{_{\IfValueT}}\mathopen{}{\left( #2 \right)}}{\IfValueT}{_{\IfValueT}}\mathopen{}{\left( #2 \right)}}{\{}{_{\{}}\mathopen{}{\left( #2 \right)}}x^2, 2\left\lvert x \right\rvert\} \le (x^2)^{\alpha} (2\left\lvert x \right\rvert)^{1-\alpha} = 2^{1-\alpha} \left\lvert x \right\rvert^{1+\alpha}$, H\"older's inequality with conjugate exponents $p$ and $q$ gives
$$
 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\left\lvert f_1 \right\rvert \phi(B)}{_{\left\lvert f_1 \right\rvert \phi(B)}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\left\lvert f_1 \right\rvert \phi(B)}{_{\left\lvert f_1 \right\rvert \phi(B)}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}
\le
2^{1-\alpha} \left\lVert f_1 \right\rVert_p  
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\left\lvert B \right\rvert^{(1+\alpha) q}}{_{\left\lvert B \right\rvert^{(1+\alpha) q}}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\left\lvert B \right\rvert^{(1+\alpha) q}}{_{\left\lvert B \right\rvert^{(1+\alpha) q}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}^{1/q}
=
2^{2/p} \left\lVert f_1 \right\rVert_p  
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{B^2}{_{B^2}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{B^2}{_{B^2}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}^{1 - 1/p}.
$$
By \cref{lem.clt_2cf.stability_bounds.variance} and \Cref{ass.clt_2cf.degeneracy},
$$
 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{B^2}{_{B^2}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{B^2}{_{B^2}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}
=
\frac{n}{4(1+\rho_n)}  
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} \mid \mathcal{D}_{-1}}{_{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} \mid \mathcal{D}_{-1}}}} 
{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} \mid \mathcal{D}_{-1}}{_{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} \mid \mathcal{D}_{-1}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}}{_{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} \mid \mathcal{D}_{-1}}{_{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} \mid \mathcal{D}_{-1}}}} 
{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} \mid \mathcal{D}_{-1}}{_{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} \mid \mathcal{D}_{-1}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} \mid \mathcal{D}_{-1}}{_{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} \mid \mathcal{D}_{-1}}}} 
{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} \mid \mathcal{D}_{-1}}{_{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} \mid \mathcal{D}_{-1}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}}{_{ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} \mid \mathcal{D}_{-1}}{_{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} \mid \mathcal{D}_{-1}}}} 
{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} \mid \mathcal{D}_{-1}}{_{\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_1} \mid \mathcal{D}_{-1}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}
=
\frac{\gamma_n}{4(1+\rho_n)}
\le
\frac{\gamma_n}{4 \underaccent{\bar}{c}},
$$
and therefore, since $\left\lVert f_1 \right\rVert_p = \left\lVert f_{{\hat{\eta}}_1}(W) \right\rVert_p \le \bar c$ by fold symmetry and $\gamma_n \le M n^{-1/(1-1/p)}$,
$$
 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\left\lvert f_1 \right\rvert \phi(B)}{_{\left\lvert f_1 \right\rvert \phi(B)}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\left\lvert f_1 \right\rvert \phi(B)}{_{\left\lvert f_1 \right\rvert \phi(B)}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}
\le
2^{2/p} \bar c \left( \frac{\gamma_n}{4 \underaccent{\bar}{c}} \right)^{1 - 1/p}
\le
C \gamma_n^{1 - 1/p}
\le
\frac{C}{n}.
$$
Substituting both bounds into \cref{thm.clt_2cf.quantitative.split} yields $\sqrt{n}  
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\left\lvert f_1 \right\rvert \phi(\Delta_1)}{_{\left\lvert f_1 \right\rvert \phi(\Delta_1)}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\left\lvert f_1 \right\rvert \phi(\Delta_1)}{_{\left\lvert f_1 \right\rvert \phi(\Delta_1)}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]} \le C / \sqrt{n}$.

Combining the three bounds with \cref{thm.clt_2cf.stein_bound.bound},
$$
d_W{\left( T_n, \mathcal{N}{\left( 0,1 \right)} \right)}
\le
\sqrt{\frac{C}{n}} + \frac{1}{\sqrt{\underaccent{\bar}{c}}} \left[ \frac{C}{\sqrt n} + \frac{2C}{\sqrt n} \right]
=
O(n^{-1/2}).
$$

<!-- proof-7: body_md -->
Write $Z_k^\circ$ for $Z_{k,1}$ with its denominator replaced by $\sqrt m\,\sigma_{{\hat{\eta}}_{k,1}}$.
After subtracting the fitted population mean from each score, write $Z_k^\circ=U_k-V_k$, where $U_k$ and $V_k$ are the primary and reference normalized sums.
Unconditionally, the four quarters are independent i.i.d. samples.
Conditional on the two training quarters, $U_1,U_2$ are fixed, while $V_1,V_2$ are independent, centered, and have variance one.
Therefore,
\begin{equation}
 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{Z_1^\circ Z_2^\circ}{_{Z_1^\circ Z_2^\circ}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{Z_1^\circ Z_2^\circ}{_{Z_1^\circ Z_2^\circ}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}=\rho_{n/2},
\qquad
 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{(Z_k^\circ)^2}{_{(Z_k^\circ)^2}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{(Z_k^\circ)^2}{_{(Z_k^\circ)^2}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}=2.
\label{eq.inference.proof.oracle}
\end{equation}

For fixed values $x_1,\dots,x_{2m}$, let $Z(x)$ denote the contrast in \cref{eq.inference.contrast} under a uniform balanced split.
If $v(x)$ is the centered vector divided by its Euclidean norm $\left\lVert \cdot \right\rVert_2$, with $v(x)=0$ for a constant vector, then
$$
Z(x)=\sqrt{\frac{2m-1}{m}}\,\epsilon^\top v(x),
$$
where $\epsilon_i$ is $1$ on the selected half and $-1$ on its complement.
The balanced-sign identities
$$
 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\epsilon_i}{_{\epsilon_i}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\epsilon_i}{_{\epsilon_i}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}=0,
\qquad
 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\epsilon_i\epsilon_j}{_{\epsilon_i\epsilon_j}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\epsilon_i\epsilon_j}{_{\epsilon_i\epsilon_j}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}=-\frac1{2m-1},
\qquad
 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\epsilon_i\epsilon_j\epsilon_\ell\epsilon_r}{_{\epsilon_i\epsilon_j\epsilon_\ell\epsilon_r}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\epsilon_i\epsilon_j\epsilon_\ell\epsilon_r}{_{\epsilon_i\epsilon_j\epsilon_\ell\epsilon_r}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}
=
\frac3{(2m-1)(2m-3)}
$$
for distinct indices give
\begin{equation}
 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{Z(x)}{_{Z(x)}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{Z(x)}{_{Z(x)}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}=0,
\qquad
 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{Z(x)^2}{_{Z(x)^2}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{Z(x)^2}{_{Z(x)^2}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}=2 
\IfNoValueTF{#1}{\operatorname{\mathds{1}}} 
{\mathds{1}\mathopen{}{\left( #1 \right)}}{v(x)\ne0},
\qquad
 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{Z(x)^4}{_{Z(x)^4}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{Z(x)^4}{_{Z(x)^4}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}\le12.
\label{eq.inference.proof.balanced}
\end{equation}
The case $m=1$ follows directly.

Conditional on the training quarter, the pooled test observations are i.i.d.
The moment condition gives uniform integrability of the squared normalized scores, so truncation and the conditional law of large numbers imply
$$
 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\left\lvert 
\frac{\hat{\sigma}_{{\hat{\eta}}_{k,1}}^2}
{\sigma_{{\hat{\eta}}_{k,1}}^2}-1
 \right\rvert}{_{\left\lvert 
\frac{\hat{\sigma}_{{\hat{\eta}}_{k,1}}^2}
{\sigma_{{\hat{\eta}}_{k,1}}^2}-1
 \right\rvert}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\left\lvert 
\frac{\hat{\sigma}_{{\hat{\eta}}_{k,1}}^2}
{\sigma_{{\hat{\eta}}_{k,1}}^2}-1
 \right\rvert}{_{\left\lvert 
\frac{\hat{\sigma}_{{\hat{\eta}}_{k,1}}^2}
{\sigma_{{\hat{\eta}}_{k,1}}^2}-1
 \right\rvert}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}\to0.
$$
Conditioning on the pooled scores and randomizing their balanced split then gives
\begin{align*}
 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{(Z_{k,1}-Z_k^\circ)^2}{_{(Z_{k,1}-Z_k^\circ)^2}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{(Z_{k,1}-Z_k^\circ)^2}{_{(Z_{k,1}-Z_k^\circ)^2}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}
&\le
2 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\left( 
\frac{\hat{\sigma}_{{\hat{\eta}}_{k,1}}}
{\sigma_{{\hat{\eta}}_{k,1}}}-1
 \right)^2}{_{\left( 
\frac{\hat{\sigma}_{{\hat{\eta}}_{k,1}}}
{\sigma_{{\hat{\eta}}_{k,1}}}-1
 \right)^2}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\left( 
\frac{\hat{\sigma}_{{\hat{\eta}}_{k,1}}}
{\sigma_{{\hat{\eta}}_{k,1}}}-1
 \right)^2}{_{\left( 
\frac{\hat{\sigma}_{{\hat{\eta}}_{k,1}}}
{\sigma_{{\hat{\eta}}_{k,1}}}-1
 \right)^2}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}\\
&\le
2 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\left\lvert 
\frac{\hat{\sigma}_{{\hat{\eta}}_{k,1}}^2}
{\sigma_{{\hat{\eta}}_{k,1}}^2}-1
 \right\rvert}{_{\left\lvert 
\frac{\hat{\sigma}_{{\hat{\eta}}_{k,1}}^2}
{\sigma_{{\hat{\eta}}_{k,1}}^2}-1
 \right\rvert}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{\left\lvert 
\frac{\hat{\sigma}_{{\hat{\eta}}_{k,1}}^2}
{\sigma_{{\hat{\eta}}_{k,1}}^2}-1
 \right\rvert}{_{\left\lvert 
\frac{\hat{\sigma}_{{\hat{\eta}}_{k,1}}^2}
{\sigma_{{\hat{\eta}}_{k,1}}^2}-1
 \right\rvert}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}
\to0.
\end{align*}
When the sample variance is zero, both contrasts are zero.
By Cauchy--Schwarz and \cref{eq.inference.proof.oracle},
$$
 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{Z_{1,1}Z_{2,1}}{_{Z_{1,1}Z_{2,1}}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{Z_{1,1}Z_{2,1}}{_{Z_{1,1}Z_{2,1}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}=\rho_{n/2}+o(1).
$$

Put $q_n= 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{Z_{1,1}Z_{2,1}\mid\mathcal{D}}{_{Z_{1,1}Z_{2,1}\mid\mathcal{D}}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{Z_{1,1}Z_{2,1}\mid\mathcal{D}}{_{Z_{1,1}Z_{2,1}\mid\mathcal{D}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}$.
Applying \cref{eq.inference.proof.balanced} conditionally on the data and the training subset gives
$$
 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{Z_{k,1}^2\mid\mathcal{D}}{_{Z_{k,1}^2\mid\mathcal{D}}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{Z_{k,1}^2\mid\mathcal{D}}{_{Z_{k,1}^2\mid\mathcal{D}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}\le2,
\qquad
 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{Z_{k,1}^4\mid\mathcal{D}}{_{Z_{k,1}^4\mid\mathcal{D}}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{Z_{k,1}^4\mid\mathcal{D}}{_{Z_{k,1}^4\mid\mathcal{D}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}\le12,
$$
and hence $|q_n|\le2$ and $ 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{Z_{1,1}Z_{2,1}\mid\mathcal{D}}{_{Z_{1,1}Z_{2,1}\mid\mathcal{D}}}} 
{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{Z_{1,1}Z_{2,1}\mid\mathcal{D}}{_{Z_{1,1}Z_{2,1}\mid\mathcal{D}}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}\le12$.
Conditional independence of the repetitions and the fact that projection onto $[-1,1]$ cannot increase the distance to $\rho_n$ yield
\begin{align*}
 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{(\widehat{\rho}_n-\rho_n)^2}{_{(\widehat{\rho}_n-\rho_n)^2}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{(\widehat{\rho}_n-\rho_n)^2}{_{(\widehat{\rho}_n-\rho_n)^2}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}
&\le
\frac{12}{B}
+
 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{q_n}{_{q_n}}} 
{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{q_n}{_{q_n}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}
+
\left\lbrace  
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{q_n}{_{q_n}}} 
{\operatorname{\mathbb{E}}\IfValueT{q_n}{_{q_n}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}-\rho_n \right\rbrace^2\\
&=
\frac{12}{B}
+
 
\IfNoValueTF{#2}{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{q_n}{_{q_n}}} 
{\operatorname{Var}\IfValueT{q_n}{_{q_n}}\mathopen{}\left[ #2 \right]}
+
\left\lbrace \rho_{n/2}-\rho_n+o(1) \right\rbrace^2
\to0,
\end{align*}
where the last term vanishes because $\rho_{n/2}$ and $\rho_n$ both converge to $\rho$.

The same moment argument gives
$\hat{\sigma}_{{\hat{\eta}}_k}/\sigma_{{\hat{\eta}}_k}\overset{p}{\to}1$
for the original fits.
Since $\rho>-1$, $1+\rho_n$ is bounded away from zero for all large $n$.
Consequently, \cref{eq.inference.scale_balance} and correlation consistency imply
\begin{align*}
\frac{\hat{\theta}_{\hat{\eta}}-\theta_{\hat{\eta}}}
{\widehat{\se}{\left( \hat{\theta}_{\hat{\eta}} \right)}}
&=
\frac{
\sqrt{n/2}\sum_{k=1}^2
(\hat{\theta}_{{\hat{\eta}}_k}-\theta_{{\hat{\eta}}_k})/\sigma_{{\hat{\eta}}_k}
}{
\sqrt{2(1+\rho_n)}
}
+o_p(1)\\
&\leadsto\mathcal{N}{\left( 0,1 \right)},
\end{align*}
where the last step uses \Cref{thm.clt_2cf}.
Continuity of the normal distribution proves the coverage statement.
<!-- end proof-7 -->
